# Motion Library — All 327 Prompts (English)

English version of `prompts.md`, translated from the Chinese original. Animated examples: see `examples_en/` and the runnable site in `site/` (use its 中文 / EN toggle).

## Text (text) — 105

### 001 · Lightning Strike — 閃電砸落

Slams down hard with a lightning flash

- Component: Text (`text`)
- Timing: Entrance
- Tech: CSS
- Uses: Headline · Hero, Emphasis
- Style / keywords: Impact Entrance
- Tag: Impact · Opening
- Example text: "Thunder" (stage background: dark)
- Added: 2026-10-15
- Full example: [examples_en/text/001-lightning-strike.md](examples_en/text/001-lightning-strike.md)

**AI prompt (English copy format):**

```text
"Lightning Strike (閃電砸落)" motion effect: The screen first flashes brightly twice and a lightning bolt appears; the text slams down instantly from 2x size and shrinks back to its original size, trembling slightly on landing, then keeps a soft outer glow. The whole sequence lasts about 2.4 seconds, with a fast, explosive rhythm.
Colors: please match the website's existing theme colors and style yourself; do not specify any additional colors.
```

(GSAP: one ScrollTrigger per section, start "top center", end "bottom center", onToggle toggles active; the progress bar uses scaleY with scrub:true; clicking uses gsap.to(scroller,{scrollTop}) or ScrollToPlugin)

(JS requestAnimationFrame: position lerps toward the target; speed = the length of this frame's displacement, angle = atan2(dy,dx); transform = translate → rotate(angle) → scale(1 + speed coefficient, 1 − speed coefficient × 0.45), and the speed coefficient itself is also lerped to avoid jitter)
